     9	import type { InstanceInfo } from "@/state/store";
    10	
    11	// First-run onboarding: who you are (email), what's installed (live engine
    12	// checks from the harness), what the app may use (TCC), then an optional
    13	// phone setup that can always be resumed from Settings → Phone.
    14	// Every check is skippable — onboarding must never brick the app.
    15	
    16	type InstanceRow = InstanceInfo;
    17	
    18	function StatusRow({
    19	  ok,
    20	  warn,
    21	  title,
    22	  detail,
    23	  mark,
    24	  children,
    25	}: {
    26	  ok: boolean;
    27	  warn?: boolean;
    28	  title: string;
    29	  detail?: string;
    30	  mark?: ReactNode;
    31	  children?: ReactNode;
    32	}) {
    33	  return (
    34	    <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl bg-card p-3.5">
    35	      <span
    36	        className={`mt-0.5 flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
    37	          ok ? "bg-success/15 text-success" : warn ? "bg-warning/15 text-warning" : "bg-raised text-ink-secondary"
    38	        }`}
    39	      >
    40	        {ok ? <Check size={14} /> : <AlertTriangle size={13} />}
    41	      </span>
    42	      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
    43	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-medium text-ink">
    44	          {mark}
    45	          <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{title}</span>
    46	        </div>
    47	        {detail && <div className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-secondary">{detail}</div>}
    48	        {children}
    49	      </div>
    50	    </div>
    51	  );
    52	}
    53	
    54	/** One engine on the setup screen: what it's called, what the harness
    55	 * found, and the one-liner to show when it's good to go. Ready states get
    56	 * a sentence; anything the user has to act on gets the shared setup UI, so
    57	 * the instructions come from the driver and are correct for this platform. */
    58	interface EngineEntry {
    59	  instance: InstanceRow;
    60	  label: string;
    61	  readyNote: string;
    62	}
    63	
    64	function engineReady(instance: InstanceRow): boolean {
    65	  return (
    66	    instance.snapshot.state === "available" &&
    67	    (instance.access === "custom" || instance.snapshot.authenticated !== false)
    68	  );
    69	}
    70	
    71	function engineTitle({ instance, label }: EngineEntry): string {
    72	  const version = instance?.snapshot.version ? ` · ${instance.snapshot.version.split(" ")[0]}` : "";
    73	  return `${label}${version}`;
    74	}
    75	
    76	/** A ready engine needs no attention: a small tile in the grid, so five
    77	 * engines don't read as one long list where the good news and the setup
    78	 * work look the same. */
    79	function ReadyTile(entry: EngineEntry) {
    80	  return (
    81	    <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-xl bg-card p-3">
    82	      <ProviderMark driverKind={entry.instance.driverKind} size={17} />
    83	      <div className="min-w-0">
    84	        <div className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-medium text-ink">{engineTitle(entry)}</div>
    85	        <div className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] leading-snug text-ink-secondary">{entry.readyNote}</div>
    86	      </div>
    87	    </div>
    88	  );
    89	}
    90	
    91	/** An engine that still needs installing or signing in keeps the full-width
    92	 * row: the command box and terminal button need the room. */
    93	function SetupRow(entry: EngineEntry) {
    94	  return (
    95	    <StatusRow
    96	      ok={false}
    97	      warn
    98	      title={engineTitle(entry)}
    99	      mark={<ProviderMark driverKind={entry.instance.driverKind} size={16} />}
   100	    >
   101	      <EngineSetup
   102	        instance={entry.instance}
   103	        className="mt-0.5"
   104	        intent={entry.instance.access === "custom" ? "inject" : "cloud"}
   105	      />
   106	    </StatusRow>
   107	  );
   108	}
   109	
   110	export function Onboarding({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
   111	  const { capabilities } = useDesktopCapabilities();
   112	  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
   113	  const [name, setName] = useState("");
   114	  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
   115	  const [instances, setInstances] = useState<InstanceRow[] | null>(null);
   116	  const [perms, setPerms] = useState<{ mic: string } | null>(null);
   117	  const valid = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(email.trim());
   118	
   119	  const saveProfile = () => {
   120	    identifyEmail(email.trim().toLowerCase());
   121	    // Our own list. Deliberately not awaited: Sendlane being slow or down must
   122	    // never hold someone at the welcome screen.
   123	    void fetch("/api/subscribe", {
   124	      method: "POST",
   125	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
   126	      body: JSON.stringify({ email: email.trim().toLowerCase(), name: name.trim() }),
   127	    }).catch(() => {});
   128	    // persisted server-side (~/.murage/config.json) — the sidebar
   129	    // footer reads it back through /api/config
   130	    void fetch("/api/config", {
   131	      method: "PUT",
   132	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
   133	      body: JSON.stringify({ profile: { name: name.trim(), email: email.trim().toLowerCase() } }),
   134	    }).catch(() => {});
   135	    setStep(1);
   136	  };
   137	
   138	  useEffect(() => {
   139	    track("onboarding_step", { step });
   140	  }, [step]);
   141	
   142	  useEffect(() => {
   143	    if (step !== 1) return;
   144	    let active = true;
   145	    let latestRequest = 0;
   146	    const refresh = () => {
   147	      const request = ++latestRequest;
   148	      fetch("/api/instances")
   149	        .then((r) => r.json())
   150	        .then((d) => active && request === latestRequest && setInstances(d.instances ?? []))
   151	        .catch(() => active && request === latestRequest && setInstances([]));
   152	    };
   153	    refresh();
   154	    window.addEventListener("focus", refresh);
   155	    return () => {
   156	      active = false;
   157	      window.removeEventListener("focus", refresh);
   158	    };
   159	  }, [step]);
   160	
   161	  useEffect(() => {
   162	    if (step === 2 && capabilities.dictation.available) {
   163	      const poll = () => window.muragebox?.permStatus?.().then(setPerms).catch(() => {});
   164	      poll();
   165	      // keep polling — the user may grant in System Settings and come back
   166	      const t = setInterval(poll, 2000);
   167	      return () => clearInterval(t);
   168	    }
   169	  }, [step, capabilities.dictation.available]);
   170	
   171	  const finish = () => {
   172	    track("onboarding_completed", {
   173	      engines_available: instances?.filter((i) => i.snapshot.state === "available").length ?? -1,
   174	      mic: perms?.mic ?? "n/a",
   175	    });
   176	    setEmailGateDone("submitted");
   177	    onDone();
   178	  };
   179	
   180	  const engines: EngineEntry[] = (instances ?? [])
   181	    .filter((instance) => instance.install)
   182	    .map((instance) => ({
   183	      instance,
   184	      label: instance.displayName,
   185	      readyNote:
   186	        instance.access === "custom"
   187	          ? "Installed — ready for a local model."
   188	          : "Installed — ready to power bots.",
   189	    }));
   190	  const readyEngines = engines.filter((e) => engineReady(e.instance));
   191	  const setupEngines = engines.filter((e) => !engineReady(e.instance));
   192	
   193	  return (
   194	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-app p-8">
   195	      {/* the engines step lays tiles out two across, so it gets more room —
   196	          but never more than the window: the panel caps at the viewport and
   197	          the engine list scrolls inside it, so the header and Continue stay
   198	          put and nothing runs into the edges */}
   199	      <div
   200	        className={`flex max-h-full w-full flex-col rounded-2xl border border-hairline/40 bg-panel p-8 ${step === 1 ? "max-w-[680px]" : step === 3 ? "max-w-[620px]" : "max-w-[460px]"}`}
   201	      >
   202	        {step === 0 && (
   203	          <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
   204	            <img
   205	              src="/murage-logo.png"
   206	              alt="Murage"
   207	              className="mb-7 h-14 w-auto"
   208	              draggable={false}
   209	            />
   210	            <EmberAvatar color="orange" state="happy" size={72} />
   211	            <h1 className="mt-4 text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Welcome to Murage</h1>
   212	            <p className="mt-1.5 text-center text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink-secondary">
   213	              One desktop, every AI engine doing REAL work on their OWN computer.
   214	              Tell us who you are and we&rsquo;ll let you know when big things ship.
   215	            </p>
   216	            <input
   217	              autoFocus
   218	              type="text"
   219	              value={name}
   220	              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
   221	              placeholder="Your name"
   222	              className="mt-5 w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2.5 text-[15px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   223	            />
   224	            <input
   225	              type="email"
   226	              value={email}
   227	              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
   228	              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && valid && saveProfile()}
   229	              placeholder="you@example.com"
   230	              className="mt-3 w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2.5 text-[15px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   231	            />
   232	            <button
   233	              onClick={saveProfile}
   234	              disabled={!valid}
   235	              className="mt-3 w-full rounded-lg bg-accent py-2.5 text-[15px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
   236	            >
   237	              Continue
   238	            </button>
   239	            <button
   240	              onClick={() => {
   241	                track("email_skipped");
   242	                setStep(1);
   243	              }}
   244	              className="mt-3 text-[12px] text-ink-secondary hover:text-ink"
   245	            >
   246	              Maybe later
   247	            </button>
   248	          </div>
   249	        )}
   250	
   251	        {step === 1 && (
   252	          <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col">
   253	            <h1 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">Your engines</h1>
   254	            <p className="mt-1 text-[13.5px] text-ink-secondary">
   255	              Bots run on AI tools installed on this computer — here&rsquo;s what we found.
   256	            </p>
   257	            <div className="mt-4 flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-2.5 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
   258	              {!instances ? (
   259	                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-6 text-ink-secondary">
   260	                  <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> Checking…
   261	                </div>
   262	              ) : (
   263	                <>
   264	                  {readyEngines.length > 0 && (
   265	                    <>
   266	                      <div className="text-[11.5px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary">Ready</div>
   267	                      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
   268	                        {readyEngines.map((e) => (
   269	                          <ReadyTile key={e.label} {...e} />
   270	                        ))}
   271	                      </div>
   272	                    </>
   273	                  )}
   274	                  {setupEngines.length > 0 && (
   275	                    <>
   276	                      <div className={`text-[11.5px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary ${readyEngines.length ? "mt-2" : ""}`}>
   277	                        Needs setup
   278	                      </div>
   279	                      {setupEngines.map((e) => (
   280	                        <SetupRow key={e.label} {...e} />
   281	                      ))}
   282	                    </>
   283	                  )}
   284	                </>
   285	              )}
   286	            </div>
   287	            <button
   288	              onClick={() => setStep(capabilities.dictation.available ? 2 : 3)}
   289	              className="mt-5 w-full shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-accent py-2.5 text-[15px] font-medium text-white"
   290	            >
   291	              Continue
   292	            </button>
   293	          </div>
   294	        )}
   295	
   296	        {step === 2 && (
   297	          <div className="flex flex-col">
   298	            <h1 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">Permissions</h1>
   299	            <p className="mt-1 text-[13.5px] text-ink-secondary">
   300	              Optional, and only ever used when you ask for the feature.
   301	            </p>
   302	            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
   303	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl bg-card p-3.5">
   304	                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
   305	                  <Mic size={18} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-ink-secondary" />
   306	                  <div>
   307	                    <div className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink">Microphone & speech</div>
   308	                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-secondary">
   309	                      Voice dictation into the composer, transcribed on-device.
   310	                    </div>
   311	                  </div>
   312	                </div>
   313	                {perms?.mic === "granted" ? (
   314	                  <Check size={16} className="shrink-0 text-success" />
   315	                ) : perms?.mic === "denied" || perms?.mic === "restricted" ? (
   316	                  <button
   317	                    onClick={() => window.muragebox?.permOpenSettings?.("mic")}
   318	                    className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-raised px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover"
   319	                  >
   320	                    Open Settings
   321	                  </button>
   322	                ) : (
   323	                  <button
   324	                    onClick={() =>
   325	                      window.muragebox?.permRequestMic?.().then(() => window.muragebox?.permStatus?.().then(setPerms))
   326	                    }
   327	                    className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-raised px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover"
   328	                  >
   329	                    Enable
   330	                  </button>
   331	                )}
   332	              </div>
   333	              {/* Screen Recording deliberately has no row here: macOS 15+
   334	                  makes a pre-grant unreliable (per-process status caching,
   335	                  helper misattribution, periodic re-prompts) — the OS flow
   336	                  triggers on the first real capture in the Computer panel,
   337	                  which is the moment the user has context for the dialog. */}
   338	            </div>
   339	            <button onClick={() => setStep(3)} className="mt-5 w-full rounded-lg bg-accent py-2.5 text-[15px] font-medium text-white">
   340	              Continue
   341	            </button>
   342	            <button onClick={() => setStep(3)} className="mt-3 text-[12px] text-ink-secondary hover:text-ink">
   343	              Skip for now
   344	            </button>
   345	          </div>
   346	        )}
   347	
   348	        {step === 3 && (
   349	          <PhoneSetupFlow
   350	            variant="onboarding"
   351	            profileEmail={email}
   352	            onSkip={() => {
   353	              track("phone_setup_skipped");
   354	              finish();
   355	            }}
   356	            onComplete={() => {
   357	              track("phone_setup_completed");
   358	              finish();
   359	            }}
   360	          />
   361	        )}
   362	
   363	      </div>
   364	    </div>
   365	  );
   366	}
     1	// Paste-a-key rows. Packaged Electron saves secrets in the OS-backed store;
     2	// browser development falls back to PUT /api/config. Secrets are write-only
     3	// either way — GET /api/config returns configured flags, never values.
     4	import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react";
     5	import { Check, CircleHelp, ExternalLink, Loader2, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
     6	import { api, useStore, type ConfigStatus } from "@/state/store";
     7	import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
     8	
     9	export type ConfigSection = "composio" | "box" | "opencodeGo";
    10	
    11	const SECTIONS: Record<
    12	  ConfigSection,
    13	  { body: (value: string) => unknown; flag: (config: ConfigStatus) => boolean }
    14	> = {
    15	  composio: {
    16	    body: (v) => ({ composio: { apiKey: v } }),
    17	    flag: (c) => c.composio.configured,
    18	  },
    19	  box: { body: (v) => ({ box: { token: v } }), flag: (c) => c.box.configured },
    20	  opencodeGo: { body: (v) => ({ opencodeGo: { apiKey: v } }), flag: (c) => c.opencodeGo?.configured ?? false },
    21	};
    22	
    23	const ELECTRON_CREDENTIAL: Record<ConfigSection, "composioApiKey" | "boxToken" | "opencodeGoApiKey"> = {
    24	  composio: "composioApiKey",
    25	  box: "boxToken",
    26	  opencodeGo: "opencodeGoApiKey",
    27	};
    28	
    29	const CREDENTIALS: Record<
    30	  ConfigSection,
    31	  {
    32	    label: string;
    33	    placeholder: string;
    34	    description: string;
    35	    href: string;
    36	    linkLabel: string;
    37	    optional: boolean;
    38	    warning?: string;
    39	  }
    40	> = {
    41	  composio: {
    42	    label: "Composio project key",
    43	    placeholder: "ak_…",
    44	    description: "Connect Gmail, GitHub, Slack, Notion, and other apps through your own Composio project.",
    45	    href: "https://dashboard.composio.dev",
    46	    linkLabel: "Create or copy a project key",
    47	    optional: true,
    48	  },
    49	  box: {
    50	    label: "Box API key",
    51	    placeholder: "Paste your Box API key",
    52	    description: "Give bots an isolated remote Linux computer with a desktop and terminal.",
    53	    href: "https://docs.ascii.dev/box/api-keys",
    54	    linkLabel: "Open Box API key guide",
    55	    optional: true,
    56	    warning: "Box is a paid service after its trial. Usage may incur charges.",
    57	  },
    58	  opencodeGo: {
    59	    label: "OpenCode API key",
    60	    placeholder: "Paste an OpenCode API key",
    61	    description: "Optional. Existing OpenCode Zen, Go, and other provider connections are detected automatically.",
    62	    href: "https://opencode.ai/docs/providers/",
    63	    linkLabel: "Open the OpenCode provider guide",
    64	    optional: true,
    65	  },
    66	};
    67	
    68	function CredentialHelp({ section }: { section: ConfigSection }) {
    69	  const credential = CREDENTIALS[section];
    70	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    71	  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    72	  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
    73	  const popoverId = useId();
    74	
    75	  useEffect(() => {
    76	    if (!open) return;
    77	
    78	    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event: PointerEvent) => {
    79	      if (event.target instanceof Node && !rootRef.current?.contains(event.target)) setOpen(false);
    80	    };
    81	    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    82	      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
    83	      setOpen(false);
    84	      buttonRef.current?.focus();
    85	    };
    86	
    87	    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", closeOnOutsideClick);
    88	    document.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    89	    return () => {
    90	      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", closeOnOutsideClick);
    91	      document.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    92	    };
    93	  }, [open]);
    94	
    95	  return (
    96	    <div ref={rootRef} className="relative ml-auto">
    97	      <button
    98	        ref={buttonRef}
    99	        type="button"
   100	        aria-label={`About ${credential.label}`}
   101	        aria-expanded={open}
   102	        aria-controls={popoverId}
   103	        onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
   104	        className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-ink-secondary outline-none transition-colors hover:bg-control hover:text-ink focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/70"
   105	      >
   106	        <CircleHelp size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
   107	      </button>
   108	      {open && (
   109	        <div
   110	          id={popoverId}
   111	          role="group"
   112	          aria-label={`${credential.label} help`}
   113	          className="animate-pop-in absolute right-0 z-30 mt-1.5 w-[270px] rounded-xl border border-hairline bg-panel p-3 text-left shadow-2xl"
   114	        >
   115	          <div className="text-[12px] leading-[1.45] text-ink-secondary">{credential.description}</div>
   116	          {credential.warning && (
   117	            <div className="mt-2 flex gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-warning/25 bg-warning/10 px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] leading-[1.4] text-warning">
   118	              <TriangleAlert size={13} className="mt-px shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
   119	              <span>{credential.warning}</span>
   120	            </div>
   121	          )}
   122	          <a
   123	            href={credential.href}
   124	            target="_blank"
   125	            rel="noopener noreferrer"
   126	            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
   127	            className="mt-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-accent hover:underline focus-visible:rounded-sm focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/70"
   128	          >
   129	            {credential.linkLabel}
   130	            <ExternalLink size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
   131	          </a>
   132	        </div>
   133	      )}
   134	    </div>
   135	  );
   136	}
   137	
   138	export function ApiKeyRow({
   139	  section,
   140	  onSaved,
   141	}: {
   142	  section: ConfigSection;
   143	  /** Called after a successful save with the section's new configured flag. */
   144	  onSaved?: (configured: boolean) => void;
   145	}) {
   146	  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
   147	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
   148	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   149	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   150	
   151	  const configured = state.config ? SECTIONS[section].flag(state.config) : false;
   152	  const clearing = !value.trim() && configured;
   153	  const credential = CREDENTIALS[section];
   154	
   155	  const save = () => {
   156	    if (saving || (!value.trim() && !configured)) return;
   157	    setSaving(true);
   158	    setError(null);
   159	    const request = window.muragebox?.setCredential
   160	      ? window.muragebox.setCredential(ELECTRON_CREDENTIAL[section], value.trim())
   161	      : api("/api/config", {
   162	          method: "PUT",
   163	          body: JSON.stringify(SECTIONS[section].body(value.trim())),
   164	        });
   165	    request
   166	      .then((status: ConfigStatus) => {
   167	        dispatch({ type: "configStatus", config: status });
   168	        setValue("");
   169	        onSaved?.(SECTIONS[section].flag(status));
   170	      })
   171	      .catch((e) => setError(e.message))
   172	      .finally(() => setSaving(false));
   173	  };
   174	
   175	  return (
   176	    <div>
   177	      <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-secondary">
   178	        <span className={cn("size-1.5 rounded-full", configured ? "bg-success" : "bg-raised-hover")} />
   179	        <span>{credential.label}</span>
   180	        {credential.optional && (
   181	          <span className="rounded bg-control px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary">
   182	            Optional
   183	          </span>
   184	        )}
   185	        {configured && <span className="text-[11px] text-success">Connected</span>}
   186	        <CredentialHelp section={section} />
   187	      </div>
   188	      <div className="flex gap-2">
   189	        <input
   190	          type="password"
   191	          value={value}
   192	          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
   193	          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && save()}
   194	          placeholder={configured ? "••••••••  (paste to replace)" : credential.placeholder}
   195	          aria-label={credential.label}
   196	          autoComplete="off"
   197	          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   198	        />
   199	        <button
   200	          onClick={save}
   201	          disabled={saving || (!value.trim() && !configured)}
   202	          className={cn(
   203	            "flex w-[72px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg py-2 text-[13px]",
   204	            clearing
   205	              ? "bg-control text-danger hover:bg-raised-hover"
   206	              : "bg-control text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover",
   207	            "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
   208	          )}
   209	          title={clearing ? "Remove the saved key" : "Save"}
   210	        >
   211	          {saving ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : clearing ? "Clear" : <><Check size={13} />Save</>}
   212	        </button>
   213	      </div>
   214	      {error && <div className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-danger">{error}</div>}
   215	    </div>
   216	  );
   217	}
   218	
   219	/** Non-secret Docker-over-SSH target. Keys and passwords stay with SSH. */
   220	export function VpsConnection() {
   221	  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
   222	  const [alias, setAlias] = useState("");
   223	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   224	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   225	  const configured = Boolean(state.config?.vps?.configured);
   226	
   227	  useEffect(() => {
   228	    setAlias(state.config?.vps?.sshAlias ?? "");
   229	  }, [state.config?.vps?.sshAlias]);
   230	
   231	  const save = () => {
   232	    if (saving || (!alias.trim() && !configured)) return;
   233	    setSaving(true);
   234	    setError(null);
   235	    api("/api/config", {
   236	      method: "PUT",
   237	      body: JSON.stringify({ vps: { sshAlias: alias.trim() } }),
   238	    })
   239	      .then((status: ConfigStatus) => {
   240	        dispatch({ type: "configStatus", config: status });
   241	        setAlias(status.vps?.sshAlias ?? "");
   242	      })
   243	      .catch((e) => setError(e.message))
   244	      .finally(() => setSaving(false));
   245	  };
   246	
   247	  return (
   248	    <div>
   249	      <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-secondary">
   250	        <span className={cn("size-1.5 rounded-full", configured ? "bg-success" : "bg-raised-hover")} />
   251	        <span>Self-hosted VPS</span>
   252	        <span className="rounded bg-control px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary">
   253	          Optional
   254	        </span>
   255	        {configured && <span className="text-[11px] text-success">Connected</span>}
   256	      </div>
   257	      <div className="mb-1.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-ink-secondary">
   258	        SSH config alias for the Linux VPS. Murage uses your normal SSH config and agent; it does not store keys or passwords.{" "}
   259	        See the{" "}
   260	        <a
   261	          href="https://github.com/FerroxLabs/murage/blob/main/docs/byo-vps.md"
   262	          target="_blank"
   263	          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   264	          className="text-accent hover:underline"
   265	        >
   266	          setup guide
   267	        </a>{" "}
   268	        for the required SSH alias shape.
   269	      </div>
   270	      <div className="flex gap-2">
   271	        <input
   272	          type="text"
   273	          value={alias}
   274	          onChange={(e) => setAlias(e.target.value)}
   275	          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && save()}
   276	          placeholder="my-vps"
   277	          aria-label="Self-hosted VPS SSH config alias"
   278	          autoComplete="off"
   279	          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   280	        />
   281	        <button
   282	          onClick={save}
   283	          disabled={saving || (!alias.trim() && !configured)}
   284	          className={cn(
   285	            "flex w-[72px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg py-2 text-[13px]",
   286	            !alias.trim() && configured ? "bg-control text-danger hover:bg-raised-hover" : "bg-control text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover",
   287	            "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
   288	          )}
   289	          title={!alias.trim() && configured ? "Remove the saved alias" : "Save"}
   290	        >
   291	          {saving ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : !alias.trim() && configured ? "Clear" : <><Check size={13} />Save</>}
   292	        </button>
   293	      </div>
   294	      {error && <div className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-danger">{error}</div>}
   295	    </div>
   296	  );
   297	}

    18	function modelLabel(instance: InstanceInfo | undefined, model: string): string {
    19	  return instance?.models.options.find((option) => option.id === model)?.label ?? model;
    20	}
    21	
    22	function modelProvider(instance: InstanceInfo | undefined, model: string): string | undefined {
    23	  return instance?.models.options.find((option) => option.id === model)?.provider;
    24	}

    26	function engineStatus(instance: InstanceInfo): string {
    27	  if (needsCli(instance)) return "Not installed";
    28	  if (needsSignIn(instance)) return "Sign-in required";
    29	  return instance.snapshot.version ?? "Ready";

    32	function ModelRow({
    33	  option,
    34	  current,
    35	  defaultId,
    36	  onPick,
    37	}: {
    38	  option: ModelOption;
    39	  current: boolean;
    40	  defaultId: string;
    41	  onPick: () => void;
    42	}) {
    43	  return (
    44	    <button
    45	      type="button"
    46	      onClick={onPick}
    47	      className={cn(
    48	        "flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-[13px] text-ink hover:bg-control/60",
    49	        current && "bg-control",
    50	      )}
    51	    >
    52	      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
    53	        <span className="truncate">{option.label}</span>
    54	        {option.provider && (
    55	          <span
    56	            className="shrink-0 rounded bg-inset px-1.5 py-px text-[10px] text-ink-secondary"
    57	            title={`Provider: ${option.provider}`}
    58	          >
    59	            {option.provider}
    60	          </span>
    61	        )}
    62	        {option.id === defaultId && (
    63	          <span className="shrink-0 rounded bg-inset px-1.5 py-px text-[10px] text-ink-secondary">Default</span>
    64	        )}
    65	        {option.loaded && (
    66	          <span className="shrink-0 rounded bg-accent/10 px-1.5 py-px text-[10px] text-accent">Loaded</span>
    67	        )}
    68	      </span>
    69	      {current && <Check size={14} className="shrink-0 text-accent" />}
    70	    </button>
    71	  );
    72	}

    74	function ModelSearch({
    75	  value,
    76	  onChange,
    77	  onEscape,
    78	  local,
    79	}: {
    80	  value: string;
    81	  onChange: (value: string) => void;
    82	  onEscape: () => void;
    83	  local: boolean;
    84	}) {
    85	  return (
    86	    <div className="shrink-0 px-2 pb-2">
    87	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-2.5 py-1.5 focus-within:border-accent/60">
    88	        <Search size={13} className="shrink-0 text-ink-secondary" />
    89	        <input
    90	          value={value}
    91	          onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
    92	          onKeyDown={(event) => {
    93	            if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
    94	            event.stopPropagation();
    95	            onEscape();
    96	          }}
    97	          placeholder="Search models"
    98	          aria-label={local ? "Search local models" : "Search models"}
    99	          className="w-full bg-transparent text-[12.5px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:outline-none"
   100	        />
   101	      </div>
   102	    </div>
   103	  );
   104	}

   106	export function ModelPicker({
   107	  bot,
   108	  className,
   109	  contained = false,
   110	  label,
   111	}: {
   112	  bot: Bot;
   113	  className?: string;
   114	  /** Expand the menu in-flow under the trigger so it cannot overflow a
   115	   * narrow parent (the Agent profile sidebar). */
   116	  contained?: boolean;
   117	  label?: ReactNode;
   118	}) {
   119	  const { state, dispatch, refreshInstances } = useStore();
   120	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   121	  const [railId, setRailId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   122	  const [pane, setPane] = useState<"main" | "custom">("main");
   123	  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
   124	  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false);
   125	  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   126	
   127	  const selection = bot.modelSelection;
   128	  const active = state.instances.find((instance) => instance.instanceId === selection.instanceId);
   129	  const railInstance =
   130	    state.instances.find((instance) => instance.instanceId === (railId ?? selection.instanceId)) ?? state.instances[0];
   131	
   132	  useEffect(() => {
   133	    if (open) void refreshInstances();
   134	  }, [open, refreshInstances]);
   135	
   136	  useEffect(() => {
   137	    if (!open) return;
   138	    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event: MouseEvent) => {
   139	      const clickedNode = event.target instanceof Node ? event.target : null;
   140	      if (!rootRef.current?.contains(clickedNode)) setOpen(false);
   141	    };
   142	    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
   143	      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
   144	      if (query) setQuery("");
   145	      else if (pane === "custom" && railInstance?.models.options.some((option) => !option.custom)) setPane("main");
   146	      else setOpen(false);
   147	    };
   148	    window.addEventListener("mousedown", closeOnOutsideClick);
   149	    window.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
   150	    return () => {
   151	      window.removeEventListener("mousedown", closeOnOutsideClick);
   152	      window.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
   153	    };
   154	  }, [open, pane, query, railInstance]);
   155	
   156	  const resetList = () => {
   157	    setQuery("");
   158	    setShowAll(false);
   159	  };
   160	
   161	  const openFor = (instance: InstanceInfo | undefined) => {
   162	    const official = instance?.models.options.filter((option) => !option.custom) ?? [];
   163	    const selectedIsCustom = instance?.models.options.some(
   164	      (option) => option.id === selection.model && option.custom,
   165	    );
   166	    setPane(selectedIsCustom || isCustomOnly(instance) || official.length === 0 ? "custom" : "main");
   167	    resetList();
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  const selectRail = (instance: InstanceInfo) => {
   171	    setRailId(instance.instanceId);
   172	    const official = instance.models.options.filter((option) => !option.custom);
   173	    setPane(isCustomOnly(instance) || official.length === 0 ? "custom" : "main");
   174	    resetList();
   175	  };
   176	
   177	  const pick = (instance: InstanceInfo, model: string) => {
   178	    const sameInstance = instance.instanceId === selection.instanceId;
   179	    const nextSelection: ModelSelection = {
   180	      instanceId: instance.instanceId,
   181	      model,
   182	    };
   183	    if (sameInstance && selection.effort) nextSelection.effort = selection.effort;
   184	    dispatch({
   185	      type: "setModel",
   186	      botId: bot.id,
   187	      selection: nextSelection,
   188	    });
   189	    setOpen(false);
   190	  };
   191	
   192	  const official = railInstance?.models.options.filter((option) => !option.custom) ?? [];
   193	  const custom = railInstance?.models.options.filter((option) => option.custom) ?? [];
   194	  const currentModel = selection.instanceId === railInstance?.instanceId ? selection.model : undefined;
   195	  const filteredOfficial = filterCustomModels(official, query);
   196	  const compactOfficial = railInstance
   197	    ? suggestedModels(official, railInstance.models.default, currentModel, COMPACT_MODEL_COUNT)
   198	    : [];
   199	  const shownOfficial = query ? filteredOfficial : showAll ? official : compactOfficial;
   200	  const filteredCustom = filterCustomModels(custom, query);
   201	  const { pinned, rest } = partitionCustomModels(filteredCustom);
   202	  const blocked = railInstance
   203	    ? pane === "custom"
   204	      ? needsCli(railInstance)
   205	      : needsCli(railInstance) || needsSignIn(railInstance)
   206	    : false;
   207	  const canOpenCustom = Boolean(railInstance && !needsCli(railInstance));
   208	  const canReturnToOfficial = official.length > 0 && !isCustomOnly(railInstance);
   209	
   210	  const renderRow = (option: ModelOption) => (
   211	    <ModelRow
   212	      key={option.id}
   213	      option={option}
   214	      current={selection.instanceId === railInstance?.instanceId && selection.model === option.id}
   215	      defaultId={railInstance?.models.default ?? ""}
   216	      onPick={() => railInstance && pick(railInstance, option.id)}
   217	    />
   218	  );
   219	
   220	  const trigger = (
   221	    <button
   222	      type="button"
   223	      onClick={() => {
   224	        setRailId(selection.instanceId);
   225	        setOpen((wasOpen) => {
   226	          const next = !wasOpen;
   227	          if (next) openFor(state.instances.find((instance) => instance.instanceId === selection.instanceId));
   228	          return next;
   229	        });
   230	      }}
   231	      aria-expanded={open}
   232	      aria-haspopup="dialog"
   233	      className={cn(
   234	        "flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-hairline/40 bg-control/60 py-1 pl-2 pr-2.5 text-[13px] text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover",
   235	        // in a narrow chat header fold to a rounded square with just the
   236	        // provider mark; the model name rides the tooltip (a bot with no
   237	        // resolved engine keeps its label — the mark is what would hide it)
   238	        !contained && active && COMPACT_SQUARE,
   239	      )}
   240	      title={
   241	        active
   242	          ? `${active.displayName} · ${modelLabel(active, selection.model)}${
   243	              modelProvider(active, selection.model) ? ` · ${modelProvider(active, selection.model)}` : ""
   244	            }`
   245	          : selection.model
   246	      }
   247	    >
   248	      {active && <ProviderMark driverKind={active.driverKind} size={14} />}
   249	      <span className={cn("max-w-[160px] truncate", !contained && active && "@max-4xl/chathead:hidden")}>
   250	        {modelLabel(active, selection.model)}
   251	        {active && modelProvider(active, selection.model) && (
   252	          <span className="text-ink-secondary"> · {modelProvider(active, selection.model)}</span>
   253	        )}
   254	      </span>
   255	      <ChevronDown
   256	        size={14}
   257	        className={cn(
   258	          "text-ink-secondary transition-transform",
   259	          open && "rotate-180",
   260	          !contained && active && "@max-4xl/chathead:hidden",
   261	        )}
   262	      />
   263	    </button>
   264	  );
   265	
   266	  return (
   267	    <div ref={rootRef} className={cn(contained ? "w-full" : "relative", className)}>
   268	      {contained ? (
   269	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
   270	          {label}
   271	          {trigger}
   272	        </div>
   273	      ) : (
   274	        trigger
   275	      )}
   276	
   277	      {open && (
   278	        <div
   279	          data-model-picker-content
   280	          role="dialog"
     1	// Custom picker: search the live inject list, and pin models the host
     2	// already has in memory so the user can pick them without scrolling.
     3	
     4	export function filterCustomModels<T extends { id: string; label: string; provider?: string }>(
     5	  options: readonly T[],
     6	  query: string,
     7	): T[] {
     8	  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
     9	  if (!needle) return [...options];
    10	  return options.filter(
    11	    (option) =>
    12	      option.label.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
    13	      option.id.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
    14	      (option.provider?.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ?? false),
    15	  );
    16	}
    17	
    18	export function partitionCustomModels<T extends { id: string; loaded?: boolean }>(
    19	  options: readonly T[],
    20	) {
    21	  const pinned: T[] = [];
    22	  const rest: T[] = [];
    23	  for (const option of options) {
    24	    if (option.loaded) pinned.push(option);
    25	    else rest.push(option);
    26	  }
    27	  return { pinned, rest };
    28	}
    29	
    30	/** Keep the active and default choices visible, then fill the compact list
    31	 * from catalog order. Catalog order is provider-owned and already reflects
    32	 * its preferred/current models. */
    33	export function suggestedModels<T extends { id: string }>(
    34	  options: readonly T[],
    35	  defaultId: string,
    36	  currentId: string | undefined,
    37	  limit = 5,
    38	): T[] {
    39	  const picked: T[] = [];
    40	  const seen = new Set<string>();
    41	  const add = (option: T | undefined) => {
    42	    if (!option || seen.has(option.id) || picked.length >= limit) return;
    43	    seen.add(option.id);
    44	    picked.push(option);
    45	  };
    46	  add(currentId ? options.find((option) => option.id === currentId) : undefined);
    47	  add(options.find((option) => option.id === defaultId));
    48	  for (const option of options) add(option);
    49	  return picked;
    50	}

     9	export type ConfigSection = "composio" | "box" | "opencodeGo";

    11	const SECTIONS: Record<
    12	  ConfigSection,
    13	  { body: (value: string) => unknown; flag: (config: ConfigStatus) => boolean }
    14	> = {
    15	  composio: {
    16	    body: (v) => ({ composio: { apiKey: v } }),
    17	    flag: (c) => c.composio.configured,
    18	  },
    19	  box: { body: (v) => ({ box: { token: v } }), flag: (c) => c.box.configured },
    20	  opencodeGo: { body: (v) => ({ opencodeGo: { apiKey: v } }), flag: (c) => c.opencodeGo?.configured ?? false },
    21	};

    25	  box: "boxToken",
    26	  opencodeGo: "opencodeGoApiKey",
    27	};

    68	function CredentialHelp({ section }: { section: ConfigSection }) {
    69	  const credential = CREDENTIALS[section];
    70	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    71	  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    72	  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
    73	  const popoverId = useId();

    75	  useEffect(() => {
    76	    if (!open) return;

    78	    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event: PointerEvent) => {
    79	      if (event.target instanceof Node && !rootRef.current?.contains(event.target)) setOpen(false);
    80	    };
    81	    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    82	      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
    83	      setOpen(false);
    84	      buttonRef.current?.focus();
    85	    };
    86	
    87	    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", closeOnOutsideClick);
    88	    document.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);

    90	      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", closeOnOutsideClick);
    91	      document.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    92	    };
    93	  }, [open]);
    94

   138	export function ApiKeyRow({
   139	  section,
   140	  onSaved,
   141	}: {
   142	  section: ConfigSection;
   143	  /** Called after a successful save with the section's new configured flag. */
   144	  onSaved?: (configured: boolean) => void;
   145	}) {
   146	  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
   147	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
   148	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   149	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   150	
   151	  const configured = state.config ? SECTIONS[section].flag(state.config) : false;
   152	  const clearing = !value.trim() && configured;
   153	  const credential = CREDENTIALS[section];
   154	
   155	  const save = () => {
   156	    if (saving || (!value.trim() && !configured)) return;
   157	    setSaving(true);
   158	    setError(null);
   159	    const request = window.muragebox?.setCredential
   160	      ? window.muragebox.setCredential(ELECTRON_CREDENTIAL[section], value.trim())
   161	      : api("/api/config", {
   162	          method: "PUT",
   163	          body: JSON.stringify(SECTIONS[section].body(value.trim())),
   164	        });
   165	    request
   166	      .then((status: ConfigStatus) => {
   167	        dispatch({ type: "configStatus", config: status });
   168	        setValue("");
   169	        onSaved?.(SECTIONS[section].flag(status));
   170	      })
   171	      .catch((e) => setError(e.message))
   172	      .finally(() => setSaving(false));
   173	  };
   174	
   175	  return (
   176	    <div>
   177	      <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-secondary">
   178	        <span className={cn("size-1.5 rounded-full", configured ? "bg-success" : "bg-raised-hover")} />
   179	        <span>{credential.label}</span>
   180	        {credential.optional && (
   181	          <span className="rounded bg-control px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary">
   182	            Optional
   183	          </span>
   184	        )}
   185	        {configured && <span className="text-[11px] text-success">Connected</span>}
   186	        <CredentialHelp section={section} />
   187	      </div>
   188	      <div className="flex gap-2">
   189	        <input
   190	          type="password"
   191	          value={value}
   192	          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
   193	          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && save()}
   194	          placeholder={configured ? "••••••••  (paste to replace)" : credential.placeholder}
   195	          aria-label={credential.label}
   196	          autoComplete="off"
   197	          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   198	        />
   199	        <button
   200	          onClick={save}
   201	          disabled={saving || (!value.trim() && !configured)}
   202	          className={cn(
   203	            "flex w-[72px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg py-2 text-[13px]",
   204	            clearing
   205	              ? "bg-control text-danger hover:bg-raised-hover"
   206	              : "bg-control text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover",
   207	            "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
   208	          )}
   209	          title={clearing ? "Remove the saved key" : "Save"}
   210	        >
   211	          {saving ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : clearing ? "Clear" : <><Check size={13} />Save</>}
   212	        </button>
   213	      </div>
   214	      {error && <div className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-danger">{error}</div>}
   215	    </div>
   216	  );
   217	}
   218	
   219	/** Non-secret Docker-over-SSH target. Keys and passwords stay with SSH. */
   220	export function VpsConnection() {
   221	  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
   222	  const [alias, setAlias] = useState("");
   223	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   224	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   225	  const configured = Boolean(state.config?.vps?.configured);
   226	
   227	  useEffect(() => {
   228	    setAlias(state.config?.vps?.sshAlias ?? "");
   229	  }, [state.config?.vps?.sshAlias]);
   230	
   231	  const save = () => {
   232	    if (saving || (!alias.trim() && !configured)) return;
   233	    setSaving(true);
   234	    setError(null);
   235	    api("/api/config", {
   236	      method: "PUT",
   237	      body: JSON.stringify({ vps: { sshAlias: alias.trim() } }),
   238	    })
   239	      .then((status: ConfigStatus) => {
   240	        dispatch({ type: "configStatus", config: status });
   241	        setAlias(status.vps?.sshAlias ?? "");
   242	      })
   243	      .catch((e) => setError(e.message))
   244	      .finally(() => setSaving(false));
   245	  };
   246	
   247	  return (
   248	    <div>
   249	      <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-secondary">
   250	        <span className={cn("size-1.5 rounded-full", configured ? "bg-success" : "bg-raised-hover")} />
   251	        <span>Self-hosted VPS</span>
   252	        <span className="rounded bg-control px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-secondary">
   253	          Optional
   254	        </span>
   255	        {configured && <span className="text-[11px] text-success">Connected</span>}
   256	      </div>
   257	      <div className="mb-1.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-ink-secondary">
   258	        SSH config alias for the Linux VPS. Murage uses your normal SSH config and agent; it does not store keys or passwords.{" "}
   259	        See the{" "}
   260	        <a
   261	          href="https://github.com/FerroxLabs/murage/blob/main/docs/byo-vps.md"
   262	          target="_blank"
   263	          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   264	          className="text-accent hover:underline"
   265	        >
   266	          setup guide
   267	        </a>{" "}
   268	        for the required SSH alias shape.
   269	      </div>
   270	      <div className="flex gap-2">
   271	        <input
   272	          type="text"
   273	          value={alias}
   274	          onChange={(e) => setAlias(e.target.value)}
   275	          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && save()}
   276	          placeholder="my-vps"
   277	          aria-label="Self-hosted VPS SSH config alias"
   278	          autoComplete="off"
   279	          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-hairline/40 bg-inset px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-secondary focus:border-hairline focus:outline-none"
   280	        />
   281	        <button
   282	          onClick={save}
   283	          disabled={saving || (!alias.trim() && !configured)}
   284	          className={cn(
   285	            "flex w-[72px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg py-2 text-[13px]",
   286	            !alias.trim() && configured ? "bg-control text-danger hover:bg-raised-hover" : "bg-control text-ink hover:bg-raised-hover",
   287	            "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
   288	          )}
   289	          title={!alias.trim() && configured ? "Remove the saved alias" : "Save"}
   290	        >
   291	          {saving ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : !alias.trim() && configured ? "Clear" : <><Check size={13} />Save</>}
   292	        </button>
   293	      </div>
   294	      {error && <div className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-danger">{error}</div>}
   295	    </div>
   296	  );
   297	}

     1	// Workspace credentials the desktop shell keeps OS-encrypted (credentials.bin
     2	// via safeStorage) instead of leaving in plaintext config.json — the same
     3	// treatment the Composio project key already gets in main.mjs. Pure functions:
     4	// main.mjs owns the fs and safeStorage plumbing, so the migration decisions
     5	// stay testable without an Electron runtime.
     6	//
     7	// One row per secret: the config.json home it migrates OUT of, the
     8	// credentials.bin field it lives in, and the env var the spawned server
     9	// prefers over the file (server/config.ts loadConfig).
    10	export const WORKSPACE_CREDENTIALS = [
    11	  { section: "xai", field: "key", name: "xaiApiKey", env: "XAI_API_KEY" },
    12	  { section: "box", field: "token", name: "boxToken", env: "BOX_TOKEN" },
    13	  { section: "tts", field: "key", name: "ttsKey", env: "MURAGE_TTS_KEY" },
    14	  { section: "imageGen", field: "key", name: "openaiImageApiKey", env: "MURAGE_OPENAI_IMAGE_KEY" },
    15	  { section: "opencodeGo", field: "apiKey", name: "opencodeGoApiKey", env: "OPENCODE_API_KEY" },
    16	];
    17	
    18	/** One boot-time sweep of config.json: move every plaintext workspace secret
    19	 * into the encrypted store and DELETE the plaintext field.
    20	 *
    21	 * Deleting (never blanking) keeps the meaning of what remains unambiguous:
    22	 *   - non-empty value  → newest user intent: overwrite the stored secret
    23	 *   - "" or absent     → no plaintext information; the store stays authoritative
    24	 *
    25	 * "" must never drop a stored secret. The packaged app's external-secret
    26	 * save path writes an empty tombstone into config.json on EVERY credential
    27	 * commit (the real value goes to credentials.bin first), so reading "" as
    28	 * "the user cleared this" deleted freshly saved keys at the next boot.
    29	 * Clearing runs through the desktop shell's credential:set handler, which
    30	 * removes the entry from the store directly before persisting the same
    31	 * tombstone — so there is no "" case in which the store should lose data.
    32	 * Running twice is a no-op, and nothing is lost if a boot dies between the
    33	 * two writes — the caller persists credentials BEFORE rewriting config, so
    34	 * the worst case re-runs the same overwrite.
    35	 *
    36	 * Inputs are treated as immutable; the changed flags tell the caller which
    37	 * file(s) actually need rewriting. Non-string junk in a field is left for
    38	 * the server's schema to reject rather than silently destroyed here. */
    39	export function migrateWorkspaceCredentials(config, credentials) {
    40	  const nextConfig = structuredClone(config ?? {});
    41	  const nextCredentials = { ...credentials };
    42	  let configChanged = false;
    43	  let credentialsChanged = false;
    44	  for (const { section, field, name } of WORKSPACE_CREDENTIALS) {
    45	    const home = nextConfig?.[section];
    46	    if (!home || typeof home !== "object" || Array.isArray(home)) continue;
    47	    if (!Object.hasOwn(home, field)) continue;
    48	    const value = home[field];
    49	    if (typeof value !== "string") continue;
    50	    const secret = value.trim();
    51	    if (secret && nextCredentials[name] !== secret) {
    52	      nextCredentials[name] = secret;
    53	      credentialsChanged = true;
    54	    }
    55	    delete home[field];
    56	    configChanged = true;
    57	  }
    58	  return { config: nextConfig, credentials: nextCredentials, configChanged, credentialsChanged };
    59	}
    60	
    61	/** Env for the spawned server: one var per stored secret, nothing else.
    62	 * The server treats each var as authoritative over its config.json field. */
    63	export function workspaceCredentialEnv(credentials) {
    64	  const env = {};
    65	  for (const { name, env: envName } of WORKSPACE_CREDENTIALS) {
    66	    const value = credentials?.[name];
    67	    if (typeof value === "string" && value) env[envName] = value;
    68	  }
    69	  return env;
    70	}

   226	// Packaged: the harness server ships in Resources (compiled JS, zero deps)
   227	// and runs on Electron's own Node via utilityProcess. It serves the built
   228	// UI too, so the window talks to one origin and there is no dev proxy.
   229	// A stray server on the default port must not brick the app — fall back to
   230	// alternate ports until one binds AND identifies as ours (the probe checks
   231	// our API shape, not just a 200).
   232	let serverProc = null;
   233	let serverReady = true;
   234	let secureCredentials = {};
   235	let secureCredentialState = null;

   237	const CREDENTIALS_FILE = path.join(app.getPath("userData"), "credentials.bin");
   238	
   239	/** Set once per launch: true when the store could not be READ, which is not
   240	 * the same as the user having saved nothing. Everything downstream — the
   241	 * server's view of "configured", and whether we may register a fresh
   242	 * installation — keys off this rather than off an empty object. */
   243	let credentialStoreUnavailable = false;
   244	
   245	async function loadSecureCredentials() {
   246	  const result = await readSecureCredentials({
   247	    exists: () => fs.existsSync(CREDENTIALS_FILE),
   248	    isAvailable: () => safeStorage.isAsyncEncryptionAvailable(),
   249	    readFile: () => fs.readFileSync(CREDENTIALS_FILE),
   250	    decrypt: (buffer) => safeStorage.decryptStringAsync(buffer),
   251	    sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
   252	  });
   253	  credentialStoreUnavailable = result.status === "unavailable";
   254	  if (credentialStoreUnavailable) {
   255	    // Deliberately loud. A silent {} here is what made a keychain hiccup
   256	    // look like "your connected apps are gone".
   257	    slog(`credential store unreadable after retries (${result.error}); saved keys are not loaded this launch`);
   258	  }
   259	  return result.credentials;
   260	}
   261	
   262	async function saveSecureCredentials(credentials) {
   263	  // A failed read means we do not know what the existing encrypted document
   264	  // contains. Never derive a replacement from that incomplete view: boot
   265	  // migrations must leave plaintext in place so a later launch can retry.
   266	  if (credentialStoreUnavailable) {
   267	    throw new Error("The operating-system credential store could not be read this launch");
   268	  }
   269	  if (!(await safeStorage.isAsyncEncryptionAvailable())) {
   270	    throw new Error("The operating-system credential store is unavailable");
   271	  }
   272	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(CREDENTIALS_FILE), { recursive: true });
   273	  const encrypted = await safeStorage.encryptStringAsync(JSON.stringify(credentials));
   274	  const temporary = `${CREDENTIALS_FILE}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
   275	  fs.writeFileSync(temporary, encrypted, { mode: 0o600 });
   276	  fs.renameSync(temporary, CREDENTIALS_FILE);
   277	}
   278	
   279	async function secureComposioConfig() {
   280	  const dataDir = process.env.MURAGE_DATA_DIR || path.join(app.getPath("home"), ".murage");
   281	  const configPath = path.join(dataDir, "config.json");
   282	  try {
   283	    const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
   284	    if (!config?.composio || typeof config.composio !== "object") return;
   285	    let changed = false;
   286	    const apiKey = config?.composio?.apiKey;
   287	    if (typeof apiKey === "string" && apiKey.trim().startsWith("ak_")) {
   288	      if (!secureCredentials.composioApiKey) {
   289	        secureCredentials.composioApiKey = apiKey.trim();
   290	        await saveSecureCredentials(secureCredentials);
   291	      }
   292	      config.composio.apiKey = "";
   293	      changed = true;
   294	    } else if (typeof apiKey === "string" && apiKey.trim()) {
   295	      config.composio.apiKey = "";
   296	      changed = true;
   297	    }
   298	    // These were the old Connect credential and endpoint. They are no longer
   299	    // read; remove them during the upgrade so an unused secret is not left in
   300	    // plaintext indefinitely.
   301	    for (const field of ["key", "url"]) {
   302	      if (Object.hasOwn(config.composio, field)) {
   303	        delete config.composio[field];
   304	        changed = true;
   305	      }
   306	    }
   307	    if (!changed) return;
   308	    const temporary = `${configPath}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
   309	    fs.writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });
   310	    fs.renameSync(temporary, configPath);
   311	  } catch (error) {
   312	    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") slog(`credential migration failed: ${error?.message ?? error}`);
   313	  }
   314	}
   315	
   316	// The remaining workspace credentials (xai/box/voice/OpenCode keys) get
   317	// the same at-rest treatment as the Composio key above. New packaged-app
   318	// saves go straight through credential:set below; this boot-time sweep also
   319	// migrates plaintext left by older versions or direct development clients.
   320	// See workspace-credentials.mjs for the exact rules.
   321	async function secureWorkspaceConfig() {
   322	  const dataDir = process.env.MURAGE_DATA_DIR || path.join(app.getPath("home"), ".murage");
   323	  const configPath = path.join(dataDir, "config.json");
   324	  try {
   325	    const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
   326	    const migrated = migrateWorkspaceCredentials(config, secureCredentials);
   327	    // credentials.bin first: if the OS store cannot take the secrets, the
   328	    // plaintext stays put and the next boot retries — losing the only copy
   329	    // is the one unacceptable outcome
   330	    if (migrated.credentialsChanged) await saveSecureCredentials(migrated.credentials);
   331	    secureCredentials = migrated.credentials;
   332	    if (!migrated.configChanged) return;
   333	    const temporary = `${configPath}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
   334	    fs.writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify(migrated.config, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });
   335	    fs.renameSync(temporary, configPath);
   336	  } catch (error) {
   337	    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") slog(`credential migration failed: ${error?.message ?? error}`);
   338	  }
   339	}

  1829	ipcMain.handle("assemblyai:set-key", async (_event, value) => {
  1830	  if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error("Unsupported credential");
  1831	  if (!(await safeStorage.isAsyncEncryptionAvailable())) {
  1832	    throw new Error("The operating-system credential store is unavailable");
  1833	  }

  1837	    else delete credentials.assemblyAiApiKey;

  1839	  });

  1847	const CREDENTIAL_PATCH = {
  1848	  composioApiKey: (value) => ({ composio: { apiKey: value } }),
  1849	  xaiApiKey: (value) => ({ xai: { key: value } }),
  1850	  boxToken: (value) => ({ box: { token: value } }),
  1851	  opencodeGoApiKey: (value) => ({ opencodeGo: { apiKey: value } }),
  1852	  ttsKey: (value) => ({ tts: { key: value } }),
  1853	  openaiImageApiKey: (value) => ({ imageGen: { key: value } }),
  1854	};
  1855	
  1856	ipcMain.handle("credential:set", async (_event, name, value) => {
  1857	  const patchFor = CREDENTIAL_PATCH[name];
  1858	  if (!patchFor || typeof value !== "string") {
  1859	    throw new Error("Unsupported credential");
  1860	  }
  1861	  if (app.isPackaged && !(await safeStorage.isAsyncEncryptionAvailable())) {
  1862	    throw new Error("The operating-system credential store is unavailable");
  1863	  }
  1864	  const secret = value.trim();
  1865	  const applyToHarness = async () => {
  1866	    // In development the server is a separately launched process, so it
  1867	    // cannot receive credentials from Electron at boot. Keep its established
  1868	    // local config path there; production always uses the encrypted store.
  1869	    const secretStorage = app.isPackaged ? "?secretStorage=external" : "";
  1870	    const response = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${SERVER_PORT}/api/config${secretStorage}`, {
  1871	      method: "PUT",
  1872	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  1873	      body: JSON.stringify(patchFor(secret)),
  1874	    });
  1875	    const body = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  1876	    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body?.error || `Could not save credential (HTTP ${response.status})`);
  1877	    return body;
  1878	  };
  1879	  if (!app.isPackaged) return applyToHarness();
  1880	
  1881	  // Commit the encrypted value before the server makes it live. The shared
  1882	  // state rolls credentials.bin back if validation/reload fails, while also
  1883	  // keeping concurrent account and provider updates serialized.
  1884	  return updateSecureCredentialDocument(
  1885	    (credentials) => {
  1886	      if (secret) credentials[name] = secret;
  1887	      else delete credentials[name];
  1888	      return credentials;
  1889	    },
  1890	    applyToHarness,
  1891	  );
  1892	});
  1893	
  1894	async function broadcastDesktopCapabilities() {
  1895	  const capabilities = desktopCapabilities({
  1896	    platform: process.platform,
  1897	    env: process.env,
  1898	    packaged: app.isPackaged,
  1899	    localConnection: await cuaReady,
  1900	  });
  1901	  for (const window of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
  1902	    if (!window.isDestroyed()) window.webContents.send("desktop:capabilities-changed", capabilities);
  1903	  }
  1904	}
  1905	
   450	    } catch {
   451	      /* cross-device or busy — fall through to a fresh dir */
   452	    }
   453	  }
   454	  for (const dir of [DATA_DIR, EVENTS_DIR, NATIVE_DIR]) mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   455	}
   456	
   457	export function loadConfig(): AppConfig {
   458	  let cfg: AppConfig = {};
   459	  try {
   460	    cfg = parseStoredConfig(parseJson(readFileSync(join(DATA_DIR, "config.json"), "utf8")));
   461	  } catch {
   462	    /* first run — env fallbacks below */
   463	  }
   464	  // Env wins over the file for every credential. The desktop shell keeps
   465	  // these secrets OS-encrypted and hands them to this process as env at
   466	  // spawn, leaving config.json without the plaintext field — so the file
   467	  // value is the dev-mode (no desktop shell) fallback, not the primary.
   468	  // Anything that saves a credential mid-session must keep process.env in
   469	  // step (syncCredentialEnv below), or the value injected at boot would
   470	  // shadow the save until the next launch.
   471	  cfg.xai = { ...cfg.xai };
   472	  if (process.env.XAI_API_KEY !== undefined) cfg.xai.key = process.env.XAI_API_KEY;
   473	  cfg.openaiCompat = { ...cfg.openaiCompat };
   474	  if (process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_API_KEY !== undefined) cfg.openaiCompat.key = process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_API_KEY;
   475	  if (process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_URL !== undefined) cfg.openaiCompat.url = process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_URL;
   476	  if (process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_MODEL !== undefined) cfg.openaiCompat.model = process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_MODEL;
   477	  if (process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDER !== undefined) cfg.openaiCompat.provider = process.env.OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDER;
   478	  cfg.composio = { ...cfg.composio };
   479	  if (process.env.COMPOSIO_API_KEY !== undefined) cfg.composio.apiKey = process.env.COMPOSIO_API_KEY;
   480	  cfg.box = { ...cfg.box };
   481	  if (process.env.BOX_TOKEN !== undefined) cfg.box.token = process.env.BOX_TOKEN;
   482	  cfg.opencodeGo = { ...cfg.opencodeGo };
   483	  if (process.env.OPENCODE_API_KEY !== undefined) cfg.opencodeGo.apiKey = process.env.OPENCODE_API_KEY;
   484	  cfg.tts = { ...cfg.tts };
   485	  if (process.env.MURAGE_TTS_KEY !== undefined) cfg.tts.key = process.env.MURAGE_TTS_KEY;
   486	  cfg.imageGen = { ...cfg.imageGen };
   487	  if (process.env.MURAGE_OPENAI_IMAGE_KEY !== undefined) cfg.imageGen.key = process.env.MURAGE_OPENAI_IMAGE_KEY;
   488	  return cfg;
   489	}
   490	
   491	/** After saveConfig() writes a credential, the running process's env must
   492	 * follow the newest value — loadConfig() prefers env, so the secret injected
   493	 * at boot would otherwise shadow the save until relaunch: the UI would show
   494	 * "saved" while every turn still used the old key. An empty string means the
   495	 * user cleared the credential, so the var is dropped and the (now empty)
   496	 * file value is authoritative again. Fields absent from the patch are
   497	 * untouched. */
   498	export function syncCredentialEnv(patch: Partial<AppConfig>): void {
   499	  const secrets: Array<[value: string | undefined, name: string]> = [
   500	    [patch.xai?.key, "XAI_API_KEY"],
   501	    [patch.openaiCompat?.key, "OPENAI_COMPAT_API_KEY"],
   502	    [patch.composio?.apiKey, "COMPOSIO_API_KEY"],
   503	    [patch.box?.token, "BOX_TOKEN"],
   504	    [patch.opencodeGo?.apiKey, "OPENCODE_API_KEY"],
   505	    [patch.tts?.key, "MURAGE_TTS_KEY"],
   506	    [patch.imageGen?.key, "MURAGE_OPENAI_IMAGE_KEY"],
   507	  ];
   508	  for (const [value, name] of secrets) {
   509	    if (value === undefined) continue;
   510	    if (value) process.env[name] = value;
   511	    else delete process.env[name];
   512	  }
   513	  // loadConfig() also prefers env for url/model/provider, so a saved value
   514	  // must follow the same set-when-truthy / delete-when-cleared rule as keys.
   515	  const settings: Array<[value: string | undefined, name: string]> = [
   516	    [patch.openaiCompat?.url, "OPENAI_COMPAT_URL"],
   517	    [patch.openaiCompat?.model, "OPENAI_COMPAT_MODEL"],
   518	    [patch.openaiCompat?.provider, "OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDER"],
   519	  ];
   520	  for (const [value, name] of settings) {
   521	    if (value === undefined) continue;
   522	    if (value) process.env[name] = value;
   523	    else delete process.env[name];
   524	  }
   525	}
   526	
   527	/** Env names of every workspace credential this process may be holding —
   528	 * injected at boot by the desktop shell or exported by a developer. Spawned
   529	 * engine CLIs must never inherit them: the one driver that consumes a given
   530	 * secret receives it through instanceConfigs() narrowing, and to every other
   531	 * child these are someone else's keys riding along in `...process.env`. */
   532	export const WORKSPACE_CREDENTIAL_ENV = [
   533	  "XAI_API_KEY",
   534	  "OPENAI_COMPAT_API_KEY",
   535	  "OPENAI_COMPAT_URL",
   536	  "BOX_TOKEN",
   537	  "OPENCODE_API_KEY",
   538	  "MURAGE_TTS_KEY",
   539	  "MURAGE_OPENAI_IMAGE_KEY",
   540	  "COMPOSIO_API_KEY",
   541	  "SENDLANE_API_KEY",
   542	  "SENDLANE_HASH_KEY",
   543	  "MURAGE_COMPOSIO_BROKER_TOKEN",
   544	  // Harness-private filesystem hints are not credentials themselves, but
   545	  // exposing them to a shell-capable agent points straight at app-owned
   546	  // state. The built-in browser master is delivered privately in memory.
   547	  "MURAGE_BROWSER_CONNECTION",
   548	  "MURAGE_USER_DATA",
   549	] as const;
   550	
   551	/** Drop every workspace credential from a child-process env (in place). */
   552	export function stripWorkspaceCredentialEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined>): void {
   553	  for (const key of WORKSPACE_CREDENTIAL_ENV) delete env[key];
   554	}

   556	/** Env names a provider CLI might read as its own billing identity. A spawned
   557	 * engine keeps only what its driver explicitly allows: a foreign key riding
   558	 * along in `...process.env` must not flip a subscription CLI onto
   559	 * pay-as-you-go billing the user never granted. */
   560	export const PROVIDER_CREDENTIAL_ENV = [

   572	  "CURSOR_AUTH_TOKEN",

   575	/** Merge a partial config into ~/.murage/config.json (secrets never
   576	 * echoed back — callers report configured-or-not booleans only). */
   577	export function saveConfig(patch: Partial<AppConfig>): void {
   578	  const p = join(DATA_DIR, "config.json");
   579	  let disk: JsonObject = {};
   580	  try {
   581	    const parsed = jsonObjectSchema.safeParse(parseJson(readFileSync(p, "utf8")));
   582	    if (parsed.success) disk = parsed.data;
   583	  } catch {
   584	    /* first write */
   585	  }
   586	  const checkedPatch = appConfigSchema.partial().parse(patch);
   587	  // A write is the durable migration point. Preserve every other raw key in
   588	  // config.json, but never write #567's mixed-case or duplicate profile ids
   589	  // back after we have successfully recognized the legacy list.
   590	  const storedProfiles = storedBrowserProfilesSchema.safeParse(disk.browserProfiles);
   591	  if (storedProfiles.success) disk.browserProfiles = storedProfiles.data;
   592	  for (const key of ["xai", "openaiCompat", "composio", "box", "opencodeGo", "tts", "imageGen", "profile", "rooms", "localVm", "features"] as const) {
   593	    const section = checkedPatch[key];
   594	    if (!section) continue;
   595	    const current = jsonObjectSchema.safeParse(disk[key]);
   596	    const merged: JsonObject = current.success ? { ...current.data } : {};
   597	    Object.assign(merged, section);
   598	    disk[key] = merged;
   599	  }
   600	  if (checkedPatch.vps !== undefined) disk.vps = normalizeVpsConfig(checkedPatch.vps);
   601	  // scalar, not a section: the merge loop above only walks objects
   602	  if (checkedPatch.language !== undefined) disk.language = checkedPatch.language;
   603	  // the whole list is the unit of change: an add or a delete arrives as the
   604	  // new list, never as a per-item merge
   605	  if (checkedPatch.browserProfiles !== undefined) {
   606	    // `partitionId` is read-only migration metadata. A rename/list replace
   607	    // from the renderer omits it, so carry it forward only for an unchanged
   608	    // canonical id. A genuinely new id always gets its own fresh partition.
   609	    const existingProfiles = new Map(
   610	      (storedProfiles.success ? storedProfiles.data : []).map((profile) => [profile.id, profile]),
   611	    );
   612	    const nextProfiles: BrowserProfile[] = checkedPatch.browserProfiles.map((profile) => {
   613	      const partitionId = existingProfiles.get(profile.id)?.partitionId;
   614	      return partitionId ? { ...profile, partitionId } : profile;
   615	    });
   616	    const routingConflict = browserProfileReplacementConflict(
   617	      storedProfiles.success ? storedProfiles.data : [],
   618	      nextProfiles,
   619	    );
   620	    if (routingConflict) throw Object.assign(new Error(routingConflict), { status: 409 });
   621	    disk.browserProfiles = nextProfiles;
   622	  }
   623	  if (checkedPatch.instances) {
   624	    const currentInstances = jsonObjectSchema.safeParse(disk.instances);
   625	    const diskInstances: JsonObject = currentInstances.success ? currentInstances.data : {};
   626	    for (const [instanceId, entry] of Object.entries(checkedPatch.instances)) {
   627	      const current = jsonObjectSchema.safeParse(diskInstances[instanceId]);
   628	      const merged: JsonObject = current.success ? { ...current.data } : {};
   629	      Object.assign(merged, entry);
   630	      diskInstances[instanceId] = merged;
   631	    }
   632	    disk.instances = diskInstances;
   633	  }
   634	  mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
   635	  writeFileAtomic(p, JSON.stringify(disk, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });
   636	}
   637	
   638	/** Set one instance's `config.cli` ("" clears the override back to the
   639	 * driver default). Creating the instance entry is fine — a config-less
   640	 * entry rides driver.defaultConfig(). Returns false for unknown instances
   250	      pickFolder?(current?: string): Promise<string | null>;
